Predict whether an event will happen or not, and earn money if you’re right.\nJoin 10M+ users trading thousands of prediction markets across finance, politics, weather, culture, sports, and more.\n\nFINANCIAL MARKETS\nDaily S&P 500, Dow, Nasdaq 100, Bitcoin, Gold, WTI oil, Company earnings\n\nECONOMIC MARKETS\nFed interest rates, Inflation (CPI), Jobs reports, GDP, Recession, Gas prices, Mortgage rates\n\nCLIMATE MARKETS\nDaily city temperatures, Hurricane strength, Tornado numbers\n\nCULTURE MARKETS\nBillboard 100, Netflix Top 10, Oscars, Grammys, #1 Hits\n\nHOW KALSHI WORKS\nKalshi is the largest federally regulated prediction exchange where you buy and sell contracts based on event outcomes. Contracts cost between 1¢ and 99¢ and can be sold anytime. At market close, each correct contract is worth $1.\n\nTRADE SPORTS\nKalshi lets you trade on real sports outcomes across football, basketball, hockey, soccer, MMA, tennis, golf, and more. \nTrade pro and college sports with highly liquid prediction markets. Trade live as games unfold, or combine multiple outcomes into one trade with Combos.\nKalshi is a Preferred Prediction Market Exchange of the NBA, bringing official NBA data to basketball markets.\n\nHOW IS KALSHI REGULATED?\nKalshi is federally regulated as a Designated Contract Market (DCM) by the Commodity Futures Trading Commission (CFTC). Kalshi’s affiliate, Kalshi Klear LLC, is a CFTC-regulated clearinghouse that holds member funds and clears trades.\n\nTRADE PERPETUALS\nPerpetual futures, or perps, let you trade the price of an asset without owning it. Go long if you expect the price to rise, or short if you expect it to fall. Perps have no expiration date, so you choose when to close your position.\nOn Kalshi, you can trade perps on crypto (Bitcoin, Ethereum, Solana, XRP), metals (gold, silver, copper), oil, and U.S. stock indexes, in the same app as your prediction markets. Gold and silver perps trade 24/7.\n\nKALSHI VS. STOCKS\nEvent contracts are direct. You trade the outcome of an event, not the future stock price of a company. In stock trading, you can be right and still lose money due to market sentiment or news. Prediction markets remove that layer.\n\nKALSHI VS. OPTIONS\nEvent contracts are simpler. Options pricing depends on multiple complex factors. Kalshi contracts reflect straightforward event probabilities. No time decay. If the event odds stay the same, contract value stays stable.\n\nHOW MUCH MONEY DO I NEED TO START?\nOpen and maintain a Kalshi account for free. Prediction markets require less capital than traditional trading, making it easier to diversify without risking large amounts.\n\n18+ only. Restrictions and eligibility requirements apply.
